== 1. Big project: 10 bands x 20 slots ==
Loaded 200 effects in 3881.42 ms; Triad latency 9284 samples
10 s of audio through 200 effects: average 9.28 ms per 256-sample block (174.1% of one core), worst 44.54 ms, peak level -31.0 dBFS
Switched on every LFO: 220 knobs now move with the song (slot mixes, band mixes, band volumes)
10 s of audio through 200 effects with every LFO on: average 9.52 ms per 256-sample block (178.5% of one core), worst 18.20 ms, peak level -15.7 dBFS
LFO cost: 2.5% more processing than the same project without LFOs
Saved (2943 KB) in 69.64 ms, reopened in 4360.79 ms with 200 effects running
Undo with 200 effects: a snapshot takes 77.92 ms; undoing a removed effect 58.84 ms (it is loaded again, the others stay as they are); undoing a slot mix 50.15 ms; redoing both 98.44 ms
Closed the 200-effect project in 32.50 ms

With AUNewPitch (AU, Apple) in band 2 (slot at 0% mix): Triad reports 4096 samples (85.33 ms); the dry sound comes out at sample 4096 (peak at 4097)
Vocal chain effects: AUGraphicEQ (AU, Apple), AUNBandEQ (AU, Apple), AUParametricEQ (AU, Apple), ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowMultiTool (AU, chowdsp)
Each case plays 8 s per buffer size at 48 kHz on an audio thread paced like an interface, with Triad's window open. Times are Triad's own work per block (CPU time); "late": the work took longer than the buffer lasts (a dropout on any machine); "paused by the system": the test machine (shared, virtual) stopped the thread for a moment, not Triad's work; "silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment. The project is saved every second meanwhile, like a DAW's autosave.

== 7. Recording through Triad: the extra-strong live test ==
The session: Graillon 3 (VST3, Auburn Sounds) in PRE; 3 bands at 250 Hz / 2.5 kHz (24 dB/oct) with AUGraphicEQ (AU, Apple), AUNBandEQ (AU, Apple), AUParametricEQ (AU, Apple), ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowMultiTool (AU, chowdsp); every LFO on (band volumes as a 60% tremolo) and an LFO linked to a knob inside an effect; Triad's window open; the project saved every second.
(a) Monitoring while you work: a knob turned, an LFO drawn, a window opened, an undo step kept... every 150 ms. "Silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment; "dropouts": output blocks that were silent while the voice was there, for any reason; "late": Triad's work took longer than the buffer lasts.
  48.0 kHz,  32 samples (0.67 ms), 30 s, 181 actions: Triad's work average 38 us (5.7% of the time), 99.9% under 309 us; late 17 of 45000, paused by the system 7; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  48.0 kHz,  64 samples (1.33 ms), 60 s, 369 actions: Triad's work average 76 us (5.7% of the time), 99.9% under 427 us; late 6 of 45000, paused by the system 17; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  48.0 kHz, 128 samples (2.67 ms), 30 s, 176 actions: Triad's work average 247 us (9.3% of the time), 99.9% under 1359 us; late 6 of 11250, paused by the system 0; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  44.1 kHz,  64 samples (1.45 ms), 20 s, 118 actions: Triad's work average 68 us (4.7% of the time), 99.9% under 352 us; late 0 of 13781, paused by the system 0; silenced 0, dropouts 1, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (24.4 ms)
  96.0 kHz,  64 samples (0.67 ms), 20 s, 123 actions: Triad's work average 60 us (9.1% of the time), 99.9% under 357 us; late 7 of 30000, paused by the system 10; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 2148 samples (22.4 ms)
(b) Layout changes while recording (48 kHz, 64 samples, one every 400 ms for 60 s): late 1 of 45000, not-a-number 0. How long each kind of change interrupted the sound:
  LIVE on or off (x13): Triad silenced 0.1 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.1 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
  add / remove a band (x27): Triad silenced 0.1 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.1 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
